\lambda_{h})\in L^{\infty}\left(0,T;\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\right)\times L^{\infty}\left(0,T;W_{f,h}\right)\times W^{1,\infty}\left(0,T;\mathbf{V}_{r,h}\right)\times W^{1,\infty}\left(0,T;W_{p,h}\right)\times W^{1,\infty}\left(0,T;\mathbf{V}_{s,h}\right)\times

W^{1,\infty}\left(0,T;{W}_{s,h}\right)\times L^{\infty}(0,T;\Lambda_{h})
of (
6.1a
)-(
6.1d
).

The proof of the following stability result is identical to the proof of Theorem
5.17
.

Lemma 6.5
.

For the solution of (
6.1a
)-(
6.1d
), assuming

(

⁢
H
.1

)

–

(

⁢
H
.3

)

as well as sufficient regularity of the data, there exists

\hat{C}(K,\kappa,\rho_{f},\rho_{s},\lambda_{p},\mu_{f},\mu_{p},\phi,\alpha_{\mathrm{BJS}},C_{K})>0
such that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{u}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}+\|p^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}+|\boldsymbol{u}^{\mathrm{S}}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{y}_{s,h}|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathrm{BJS})}+\|\boldsymbol{y}_{s,h}\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})\right)}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}

\displaystyle+\|\boldsymbol{u}^{\mathrm{S}}_{f,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,T;{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})\right)}+\|p^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}+\|p^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})\right)}+\|\lambda_{h}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,T;\Lambda_{h}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{C}\left(\|\boldsymbol{f}_{\mathrm{P}}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{\mathrm{P}}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{-1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}+\|r_{\mathrm{S}}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}(0)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\|p^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}(0)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+\|\boldsymbol{y}_{s,h}(0)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}(0)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\right).

6.3
Error analysis for the semi discrete scheme

In this section we analyze the spatial discretization error. Let
k_{f}
and
s_{f}
be the degrees of polynomials in
\mathbf{V}_{f,h}
and
W_{f,h}
, let
k_{p}
and
s_{p}
be the degrees of polynomials in
\mathbf{V}_{r,h}
and
W_{p,h}
respectively, and let
k_{s}

and
s_{s}
be the polynomial degree in
\mathbf{V}_{s,h}
and
\mathbf{W}_{s,h}
.

6.3.1
Approximation error

Let
Q_{f,h},Q_{p,h}
, and
\mathbf{Q}_{s,h}
be the
L^{2}
-projection operators onto
W_{f,h},W_{p,h}
, and
\mathbf{W}_{s,h}
respectively, satisfying:

(6.18a)

\displaystyle\left(p^{\mathrm{S}}-Q_{f,h}p^{\mathrm{S}},q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\in W_{f,h},

(6.18b)

\displaystyle\left(p^{\mathrm{P}}-Q_{p,h}p^{\mathrm{P}},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\in W_{p,h},

(6.18c)

\displaystyle\left\langle\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{Q}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{v}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right\rangle_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall\boldsymbol{v}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\in\mathbf{W}_{s,h}.

These operators satisfy the approximation properties
[
36
]
:

(6.19a)

\displaystyle\|p^{\mathrm{S}}-Q_{f,h}p^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{sf}+1}\|p^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{r_{sf}+1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}

\displaystyle 0\leq r_{s_{f}}\leq s_{f},

(6.19b)

\displaystyle\|p^{\mathrm{P}}-Q_{p,h}p^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{s_{p}}+1}\|p^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{{r_{s_{p}}+1},\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle 0\leq r_{s_{p}}\leq s_{p},

(6.19c)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{Q}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{{r}_{s_{s}}+1}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{{r_{s_{s}}+1},\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle 0\leq{r}_{s_{s}}\leq s_{s}.

The definition
\Lambda_{h}=\left.\mathbf{V}_{r,h}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\right|_{\Sigma}
implies the following approximation property
[
46
, Appendix A]

(6.20)

\displaystyle\|\lambda-Q_{\lambda,h}\lambda\|_{{-1/2},\Sigma}\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{\tilde{k}_{p}}+\frac{1}{2}}\|\lambda\|_{{r_{\tilde{k}_{p}}},\Sigma}\qquad{-1/2}\leq{r}_{\tilde{k}_{p}}\leq\tilde{k}_{p}-1/2.

Next, we consider a Stokes-like projection operator
\left(\mathbf{S}_{f,h},R_{f,h}\right):\mathbf{V}_{f}\rightarrow\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times W_{f,h}
, defined for all
\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\in\mathbf{V}_{f}
as

(6.21a)

\displaystyle a_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\mathbf{S}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}}\right)-b_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},R_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\right)

\displaystyle=a_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}}\right)

\displaystyle\forall\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}}\in\mathbf{V}_{f,h},

(6.21b)

\displaystyle b_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\mathbf{S}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle=b_{f}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle\forall q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\in W_{f,h}.

The operator
\mathbf{S}_{f,h}
satisfies the approximation property
[
29
,
4
]
:

(6.22)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{S}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{k_{f}}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{{r_{k_{f}}+1},\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}},\quad 0\leq r_{k_{f}}\leq k_{f}.

Let
\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}
be the Stokes projection onto
\mathbf{V}_{r,h}
satisfying for all
\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\in\mathbf{V}_{r}
,

(6.23a)

\displaystyle\left(\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)=\left(\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle\forall q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\in W_{p,h},

(6.23b)

\displaystyle\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}=\left\langle\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}

\displaystyle\forall\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\in\mathbf{V}_{r,h}.

We will make use of the following estimates regarding
\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}
:

(6.24a)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{k_{p}}+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{H^{r_{k_{p}}+1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)},\quad 0\leq r_{k_{p}}\leq k_{p},

(6.24b)

\displaystyle{\left\|\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\right\|_{1,\Omega_{p}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}\left\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\right\|_{1,\Omega_{p}}.

Finally, let
\mathbf{S}_{s,h}
be the Scott–Zhang interpolant from
\mathbf{V}_{s}
onto
\mathbf{V}_{s,h}
, satisfying
[
39
]
:

(6.25)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{S}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+h\left|\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{S}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{k_{s}}+1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{{r_{ks}+1},\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}},\quad 0\leq r_{k_{s}}\leq k_{s}.

6.3.2
Construction of a weakly-continuous interpolant

In this section we use the operators defined above to build an operator onto a space with weak continuity of normal velocities. Let us consider

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\left\{\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)\in\mathbf{V}_{f}\times\mathbf{V}_{r}\times\mathbf{V}_{s}:\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{S}}+\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}+\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}=0\quad\text{on}\ \Sigma\right\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{h}=

\displaystyle\left\{\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)\in\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{s,h}:b_{\Gamma}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}};\mu_{h}\right)=0,\forall\mu_{h}\in\Lambda_{h}\right\}.

We will construct an interpolation operator
I_{h}:\mathbf{U}\rightarrow\mathbf{U}_{h}
as a triple

I_{h}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)=\left(\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right),

with the following properties:

(6.26a)

\displaystyle b_{\Gamma}\left(\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}};\mu_{h}\right)

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall\mu_{h}\in\Lambda_{h},

(6.26b)

\displaystyle b_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\in W_{f,h},

(6.26c)

\displaystyle b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\in W_{p,h}.

We let
\mathbf{I}_{f,h}:=\mathbf{S}_{f,h}
and
\mathbf{I}_{s,h}:=\mathbf{S}_{s,h}
. To construct
\mathbf{I}_{r,h}
, we first consider the following Stokes auxiliary problem

\displaystyle-\boldsymbol{\Delta}\boldsymbol{\zeta}+\nabla p=\boldsymbol{0}\quad\text{and}\quad\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{\zeta}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\text{ in }\Omega_{\mathrm{P}},

(6.27)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\zeta}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{0}

\displaystyle\text{ on }\Gamma_{\mathrm{P}},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\zeta}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}

\displaystyle=\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{S}}+\left(\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}

\displaystyle\text{ on }\Sigma.

Define
\boldsymbol{w}=\boldsymbol{\zeta}+\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}
. From (
6.27
) we have

(6.28a)

\displaystyle\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{w}

\displaystyle=\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{\zeta}+\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}=\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\quad\text{ in }\Omega_{\mathrm{P}},

(6.28b)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{w}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\zeta}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}+\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}=-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\quad\text{ on }\Sigma.

We now let

(6.29)

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}=\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{w}.

Next, we verify that the operator
I_{h}=\left(\mathbf{I}_{f,h},\mathbf{I}_{r,h},\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\right)
satisfies (
6.26a
)-(
6.26c
). Property (
6.26b
) follows immediately from (
6.21b
), while, using (
6.28a
) and (
6.23a
), property (
6.26c
) follows from

\left(\nabla\cdot\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}=\left(\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{w},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}=\left(\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{w},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}=\left(\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\qquad\forall q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\in W_{p,h}.

Using (
6.28b
) and (
6.23b
), we have for all
\mu_{h}\in\Lambda_{h}
,

\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\mu_{h}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}=\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{w}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\mu_{h}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}=\left\langle\boldsymbol{w}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\mu_{h}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}=\left\langle-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\mu_{h}\right\rangle_{\Sigma},

which implies (
6.26a
).

The approximation properties of the components of
I_{h}
are the following.

Lemma 6.6
.

For all sufficiently smooth
\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}
,
\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}
, and for
0\leq r_{k_{p}}\leq k_{p}
,
0\leq r_{k_{f}}\leq k_{f}
,
0\leq r_{k_{s}}\leq k_{s}
, there holds

(6.30a)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{k_{f}}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{r_{k_{f}}+1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}},

(6.30b)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+h\left|\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}h^{r_{k_{s}}+1}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{r_{k_{s}}+1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}},

(6.30c)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}(h^{r_{k_{p}}+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{r_{k_{p}}+1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+h^{r_{k}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{r_{k_{f}}+1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}+h^{r_{k_{s}}}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{r_{k_{s}}+1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}).

Proof.

The bounds (
6.30a
) and (
6.30b
) follow immediately from (
6.22
) and (
6.25
). Next, using (
6.29
), we have

\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}=\|\boldsymbol{v}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{p}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{\zeta}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{p}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{p}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+\|\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{\zeta}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}.

Elliptic regularity for (
6.27
) implies,

(6.31)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\zeta}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\leq

\displaystyle C^{\star}(\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}})\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{S}}\|_{1/2,\Sigma}+\|(\xi_{p}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}})\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\|_{1/2,\Sigma}).

Using (
6.24b
), (
6.31
) and trace inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{\zeta}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}\|\boldsymbol{\zeta}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}(\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}})\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{S}}\|_{1/2,\Sigma}+\|(\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}})\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\|_{1/2,\Sigma})

(6.32)

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\star}\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{v}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}+\|\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{w}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\right).

A combination of (
6.31
), (
6.32
), (
6.22
), (
6.24a
), and (
6.25
) implies (
6.30c
).

6.4
Error estimates

We recall that, due to (
4.1
),
(\boldsymbol{u}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{u}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}})\in\mathbf{U}
and so we can apply the interpolant to get
(\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}},\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}},\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}})\in\mathbf{U}_{h}
for any
t\in(0,T]
. We split individual errors into approximation and discretization contributions

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{f}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\boldsymbol{u}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\boldsymbol{u}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}}=\left(\boldsymbol{u}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\right)+\left(\mathbf{I}_{f,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}-\boldsymbol{u}^{\mathrm{S}}_{f,h}\right)\coloneqq\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}+\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{r}

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{u}_{r}-\boldsymbol{u}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}=\left(\boldsymbol{u}_{r}-\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{r}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)+\left(\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{r}-\boldsymbol{u}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right):=\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}+\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{s}

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}=\left(\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)+\left(\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{y}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right):=\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}+\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{ss}

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{u}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}=\left(\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\mathbf{Q}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)+\left(\mathbf{Q}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}-\boldsymbol{u}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right):=\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss}+\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},

\displaystyle e_{fp}

\displaystyle:=p^{\mathrm{S}}-p^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}=\left(p^{\mathrm{S}}-Q_{f,h}p^{\mathrm{S}}\right)+\left(Q_{f,h}p^{\mathrm{S}}-p^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\right):=\chi_{fp}+\phi_{fp,h},

\displaystyle e_{pp}

\displaystyle:=p^{\mathrm{P}}-p^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}=\left(p^{\mathrm{P}}-Q_{p,h}p^{\mathrm{P}}\right)+\left(Q_{p,h}p^{\mathrm{P}}-p^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right):=\chi_{pp}+\phi_{pp,h},

\displaystyle e_{\lambda}

\displaystyle:=\lambda-\lambda_{h}=\left(\lambda-Q_{\lambda,h}\lambda\right)+\left(Q_{\lambda,h}\lambda-\lambda_{h}\right):=\chi_{\lambda}+\phi_{\lambda,h}.

Subtracting (
6.1a
)-(
6.1b
) from (
4.1
) and adding the resulting equation, we obtain the following error equation

\displaystyle a_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}(\mathbf{e}_{f},\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+a_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}(\mathbf{e}_{s},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{s},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{s},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+a_{\mathrm{BJS}}(\mathbf{e}_{f},\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{s};\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})

\displaystyle\quad+b^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},e_{fp}\right)+b_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}},e_{pp}\right)+b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},e_{pp}\right)+b_{\Gamma}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}};e_{\lambda}\right)-b_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{s},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)-b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\mathbf{e}_{r},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)-b^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\mathbf{e}_{f},q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-m_{\theta}(\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})-m_{\theta}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{s},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})-m_{\theta}(\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})-m_{\theta}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{s},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+m_{\phi^{2}/\kappa}(\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})

\displaystyle\quad+m_{\rho_{p}}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{ss},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{e}_{ss},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})+\left((1-\phi)^{2}K^{-1}\partial_{t}e_{pp},q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}\right)_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}=0.

Setting
\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}}=\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}=\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\boldsymbol{w}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\boldsymbol{v}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}=\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},q^{\mathrm{S}}_{h}=\phi_{fp,h},\text{ and }q^{\mathrm{P}}_{h}=\phi_{pp,h}
,
we conclude that the following terms simplify due to the properties of the projection operators (
6.18b
), (
6.26b
), and (
6.26c
):

b^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f},\phi_{fp,h}\right)=b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\phi_{pp,h}\right)=b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\chi_{pp}\right)=0,\qquad\left((1-\phi)^{2}K^{-1}\partial_{t}\chi_{pp},\phi_{pp,h}\right)=0.

Moreover, from (
6.26a
) and (
6.1c
) we have

b_{\Gamma}\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h};\phi_{\lambda,h}\right)=0.

On the other hand, by definition of
H^{-1/2}(\Sigma)

[
46
]
, there exists
\boldsymbol{w}\in\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)
such that

(6.33)

\displaystyle\langle\lambda,\boldsymbol{w}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\rangle_{\Sigma}=\|\lambda\|_{\Lambda_{h}}^{2},\quad\text{ and }\quad\|\boldsymbol{w}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}=\|\lambda\|_{\Lambda_{h}}\text{. }

This implies that

\langle\chi_{\lambda},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\rangle_{\Sigma}=\langle\chi_{\lambda},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}}\rangle_{\Sigma}=\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{\Lambda_{h}}^{2}.

Rearranging terms and using the results above, the error equation becomes

\displaystyle a_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})+a_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})

\displaystyle\quad+a_{\mathrm{BJS}}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h};\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})+m_{\rho_{p}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})

\displaystyle\quad+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})+((1-\phi)^{2}K^{-1}\partial_{t}\phi_{pp,h},\phi_{pp,h})_{\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}-m_{\theta}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})-m_{\theta}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})-m_{\theta}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})

(6.34)

\displaystyle\quad-m_{\theta}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})+m_{\phi^{2}/\kappa}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})=\mathcal{J}_{1}+\mathcal{J}_{2}+\mathcal{J}_{3}+\mathcal{J}_{4}+\mathcal{J}_{5}+\mathcal{J}_{6},

where the right-hand side terms are defined as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}:=

\displaystyle-a_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\right)+m_{\theta}(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h})+m_{\theta}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\right)+m_{\theta}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})-m_{\phi^{2}/\kappa}(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})-m_{\rho_{p}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h})

\displaystyle\quad-m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})-m_{\rho_{f}\phi}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\right)+m_{\theta}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h})-m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}:=

\displaystyle-a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\right)-a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{3}:=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{d-1}\left\langle\mu\alpha_{\mathrm{BJS}}\sqrt{Z_{j}^{-1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{\tau}_{f,j},\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{\tau}_{f,j}\right\rangle_{\Sigma},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{4}:=

\displaystyle-b^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\chi_{fp}\right)+b_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\phi_{pp,h}\right)-b_{\Gamma}\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},0;\chi_{\lambda}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{5}:=

\displaystyle-a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)-a_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)-a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{6}:=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\chi_{\lambda}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}-b_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\chi_{pp}\right).

It is important to remark that we have the equation

\displaystyle m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h})+m_{\rho_{s}(1-\phi)}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})+m_{\rho_{f}\phi}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\left(\|\sqrt{\rho_{f}\phi}(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}+\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h})\|^{2}_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}+\|\sqrt{\rho_{s}(1-\phi)}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}\|^{2}_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}\right).

Then, combining this equation with the inequalities (
5.23
) and

\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}+\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}\|^{2}_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}
, and using the coercivity of the bilinear forms, we can arrive at the following estimate

\texttt{LHS}_{\eqref{ne1}}\gtrsim\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right)+\left|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right|_{\mathrm{BJS}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}.

We proceed to bound the terms on the right-hand side in (
6.34
). Using Lemma
5.1
, Cauchy–Schwarz and Young’s inequalities, as well as (
6.33
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon_{1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right).

Next we estimate the terms involving
\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}
in
\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})
, since we do not have bounds on
\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}
in the energy norm on the left-hand side. We handle this by using inverse, Cauchy–Schwarz, and Young’s inequalities. Taking
\epsilon=\epsilon_{1}h^{2}
, we get

(6.35)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon h^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{-1}(\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2})\leq\epsilon_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}h^{-2}(\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}).

Similarly, using Cauchy–Schwarz, trace and Young’s inequalities, we obtain

\mathcal{J}_{3}\leq\epsilon_{1}\left|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right|_{\mathrm{BJS}}^{2}+C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right).

Finally, using Cauchy–Schwarz and Young’s inequality as well as (
6.33
), we can derive the bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\left(\|\chi_{fp}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\nabla\cdot\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right)+\epsilon_{1}\left(\|\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right)+C\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{-1/2,\Sigma}^{2}

(6.36)

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\left(\|\chi_{fp}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\chi_{\lambda}\|^{2}_{0,\Sigma}\right)+\epsilon_{1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right)+C\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{-1/2,\Sigma}^{2}.

Combining (
6.34
)-(
6.36
), integrating over
[0,t]
, where
0<t\leq T
, and taking
\epsilon_{1}
small enough, we can assert that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right|^{2}_{\mathrm{BJS}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})})}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\chi_{fp}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+h^{-2}\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+h^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}\right)+C\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}(0)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\epsilon_{1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}\right.

(6.37)

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}\right)+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(0)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}(0)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C\int_{0}^{t}\left(\mathcal{J}_{5}+\mathcal{J}_{6}\right)\,\mbox{d}s.

The interpolations
\boldsymbol{u}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}(0)=\mathbf{I}_{r,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{r,0},\quad\boldsymbol{u}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}(0)=\mathbf{Q}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{u}_{s,0},\quad p_{p,h}^{\mathrm{P}}(0)=Q_{p,h}p_{p,0}
and
\boldsymbol{y}_{s,h}^{\mathrm{P}}(0)=\mathbf{I}_{s,h}\boldsymbol{y}_{s,0}
, give

(6.38)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}(0)=0,\quad\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}(0)=0,\quad\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(0)=0,\quad\phi_{pp,h}(0)=0.

Next, we bound the terms on the right hand side involving
\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}
. By applying integration by parts in time, along with Cauchy–Schwarz and Young’s inequalities, Lemma
5.1
, and equation (
6.33
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{J}_{5}\,\mbox{d}s

\displaystyle=\left.a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)\right|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)\,\mbox{d}s+\left.a_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)\right|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}a_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)\,\mbox{d}s

\displaystyle\quad+a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{tt}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right)\,\mbox{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\partial_{tt}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\right)+\epsilon_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}.

Employing again Cauchy–Schwarz and Young’s inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{J}_{6}\,\mbox{d}s

\displaystyle=\left.\left\langle\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\chi_{\lambda}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}\right|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\cdot\boldsymbol{n}_{\mathrm{P}},\partial_{t}\chi_{\lambda}\right\rangle_{\Sigma}\,\mbox{d}s+\left.b_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\chi_{pp}\right)\right|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}b_{s}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h},\partial_{t}\chi_{pp}\right)\,\mbox{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\epsilon_{1}^{-1}\|\chi_{pp}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\chi_{pp}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,t;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}\right)

(6.39)

\displaystyle\quad+\|\chi_{\lambda}(t)\|_{-1/2,\Sigma}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}.

Choosing a sufficiently small
\epsilon_{1}
in (
6.38
)-(
6.39
), we derive the following bound from equation (
6.37
)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\left|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathrm{BJS})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})})}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\epsilon_{1}}\bigl(\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}+h^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+h^{-2}\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\chi_{fp}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\chi_{\lambda}(t)\|_{-1/2,\Sigma}^{2}+\|\chi_{pp}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}\bigr)+C\bigl(\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}

(6.40)

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}\chi_{pp}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,t;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\partial_{tt}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;\mathbf{H^{1}}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}\bigr).

Next, we employ the inf–sup condition (
6.3b
) with the choice
(q^{\mathrm{S}},q^{\mathrm{P}},\mu_{h})=\left(\phi_{fp,h},\phi_{pp,h},\phi_{\lambda,h}\right)
and utilize the error equation derived by subtracting (
6.1a
) from (
4.1
)

\displaystyle\|(\phi_{fp,h},\phi_{pp,h},\phi_{\lambda,h})\|_{W_{f,h}\times W_{p,h}\times\Lambda_{h}}\lesssim\sup_{\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)\in\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}}\frac{b^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\phi_{fp,h}\right)+b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\phi_{pp,h}\right)+b_{\Gamma}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\mathbf{0};\phi_{\lambda_{h}}\right)}{\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})\|_{\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sup_{\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)\in\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}}\left(\frac{-a_{f}^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\mathbf{e}_{f},\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}}\right)-a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)-a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\partial_{t}{\mathbf{e}}_{s},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)-a_{\mathrm{BJS}}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{f},\partial_{t}{\mathbf{e}}_{s};\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\mathbf{0}\right)}{\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})\|_{\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\frac{+m_{\theta}\left(\partial_{t}{\mathbf{e}}_{s},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)+m_{\theta}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)-k\left({\mathbf{e}}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)-m_{\rho_{f}\phi}\left(\partial_{t}{\mathbf{e}}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)}{\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})\|_{\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\frac{-m_{\rho_{f}\phi}\left(\partial_{t}{\mathbf{e}}_{s},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)-b^{\mathrm{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\chi_{fp,h}\right)-b_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\chi_{pp,h}\right)-b_{\Gamma}\left(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}},\mathbf{0};\chi_{\lambda_{h}}\right)}{\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{f,h}^{\mathrm{S}},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}})\|_{\mathbf{V}_{f,h}\times\mathbf{V}_{r,h}}}\right).

We treat the term
a_{f}^{\mathrm{P}}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{r},\boldsymbol{v}_{r,h}^{\mathrm{P}}\right)
similarly as in (
6.35
). Integrating over the interval
[0,T]
and applying Lemma
5.1
along with the trace inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\phi_{fp,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\phi_{\lambda,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}\lesssim\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathrm{BJS})}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}

(6.41)

\displaystyle\quad+\|\chi_{fp}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\chi_{pp}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}.

Adding (
6.40
) and (
6.41
), and taking

\epsilon_{2}
small enough, and then
\epsilon_{1}
small enough, gives

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ss,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\left|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\right|_{L^{2}(0,T;{\mathrm{BJS}})}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{r,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}})})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{f,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}+\|\phi_{fp,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}))}^{2}+\|\phi_{pp,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\phi_{\lambda,h}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\bigl(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}_{s,h}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}+h^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+h^{-2}\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{H}^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{ss}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right))}^{2}+\|\chi_{fp}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{S}}\right))}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\chi_{\lambda}(t)\|_{-1/2,\Sigma}+\|\chi_{pp}(t)\|_{0,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}(t)\|_{1,\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{r}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}\left(0,t;H^{1}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}+\|\partial_{t}\chi_{pp}\|_{L^{2}\left(0,t;L^{2}\left(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}\right)\right)}^{2}+\|\partial_{tt}\boldsymbol{\chi}_{s}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}+\|\chi_{pp}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\mathrm{P}}))}^{2}+\|\chi_{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{-1/2}(\Sigma))}^{2}\bigr).

Grönwall’s and triangle inequalities alongside the approximation properties (
6.19a
)-(
6.19c
), (
6.20
), and (
6.30a
)-(
6.30c
), imply the following result.

Theorem 6.7
.

Assuming

(

⁢
H
.1

)

-

(

⁢
H
.3

)

and sufficient smoothness for the solution of (
4.1
), the solution of (
6.1a
)-(
